rule (modulo E) Verifier_Challenge:
   [ Fr( ~n ) ] --> [ VChal( ~n ), Out( ~n ) ]

  /* has exactly the trivial AC variant */

rule (modulo E) Holder_Present:
   [ !Sk( 'a10_kbjwt', t, k ), In( n ) ]
  --[ Presented( n ) ]->
   [ Out( <<'kb', n>, sign(<'kb', n>, k)> ) ]

  /* has exactly the trivial AC variant */

rule (modulo E) Accept_Presentation_main:
   [ !Acc( 'a10_kbjwt', pkD, 'main' ), VChal( n ), In( <<'kb', n>, s> ) ]
  --[ Eq( verify(s, <'kb', n>, pkD), true ), AcceptPres( n ) ]->
   [ ]

  /*
  rule (modulo AC) Accept_Presentation_main:

    --[ Eq( z, true ), AcceptPres( n ) ]->
     [ ]
    variants (modulo AC)
    1. n     = n.11
       pkD   = pkD.12
       s     = s.13
       z     = verify(s.13, <'kb', n.11>, pkD.12)
    
    2. n     = n.24
       pkD   = pk(x.46)
       s     = sign(<'kb', n.24>, x.46)
       z     = true

   [
   !Acc( 'a10_kbjwt', pkD, 'alt' ),
   !ExpVal( 'a05_meta', 'a10_kbjwt', 'none' ), VChal( n ),
   In( <<'kb', n>, s> )

lemma executable:
  exists-trace "∃ n #p #j. (Presented( n ) @ #p) ∧ (AcceptPres( n ) @ #j)"
/*
guarded formula characterizing all satisfying traces:
"∃ n #p #j. (Presented( n ) @ #p) ∧ (AcceptPres( n ) @ #j)"
*/
by sorry

lemma G2_presentation_unforgeability:
  all-traces
  "∀ n #j.
    (AcceptPres( n ) @ #j) ⇒ (∃ #p. (Presented( n ) @ #p) ∧ (#p < #j))"
/*
guarded formula characterizing all counter-examples:
"∃ n #j.
  (AcceptPres( n ) @ #j) ∧ ∀ #p. (Presented( n ) @ #p) ⇒ ¬(#p < #j)"
*/
simplify
solve( AcceptPres( n ) @ #j )
  case Accept_Presentation_alt
  solve( !Acc( 'a10_kbjwt', pk(x), 'alt' ) ▶₀ #j )
    case AccIntro_a07_kimlik__a10_kbjwt_alt_case_01
    solve( !ExpVal( 'a05_meta', 'a10_kbjwt', 'none' ) ▶₁ #j )
      case ExpAcc_a05_meta__a10_kbjwt_alt_case_01
      solve( VChal( n ) ▶₂ #j )
        case Verifier_Challenge
        solve( !KU( sign(<'kb', ~n>, x) ) @ #vk.4 )
          case Holder_Present_case_1
          by contradiction /* from formulas */
        next
          case Holder_Present_case_2
          by contradiction /* from formulas */
        next
          case c_sign
          solve( !KU( sign(<'exp', 'a02_lote_pid', 'a07_kimlik', 'none'>, ~km)
                 ) @ #vk.58 )
            case c_sign
            by solve( !KU( ~km ) @ #vk.81 )
          qed
        qed
      qed
    next
      case ExpAcc_a05_meta__a10_kbjwt_alt_case_02
      solve( VChal( n ) ▶₂ #j )
        case Verifier_Challenge
        solve( !KU( sign(<'kb', ~n>, x) ) @ #vk.4 )
          case Holder_Present_case_1
          by contradiction /* from formulas */
